# Excalidraw Data

## Text Elements
We can't go from dB to dBFS directly
We need a microphone input that later digitalizes
the signal, and we need the V equivalent
to 0 dBFS
Modern systems that use the SMPTE RP155
will always use that 0 dbFS are 24 dB ^K0NpFKKq

Usually, when it digitally clips, leaving it there for a long time
will burn the speaker.
Though most modern systems can handle clipping, though for a short time usually. The Wpeak can even be lower than the clipping W, leading to speakers burning even without clipping ^m2NYjIeV

THe lower the frequency, the more constructive the waves tend
to be at longer distances (larger wavelengths) ^fhy5UP7q
